# Rulings index

Generated by `cargo jackioh spec index` from the notes in `rulings/`, and rewritten on `main` every day by `spec fold` (`.github/workflows/spec-fold.yml`), so a ruling merged since is in `rulings/` and not here yet: edit a note, never this file.

One line per ruling: its id, its title (the question it answers), its note and what it touches (the note's `cards`: the sections, cards and rulings it affects), so a search of this file for a section or a card lists the rulings on it by title (`README.md`, Looking things up). The tests that prove a ruling are in its note (`proven_in`).

| R2940001 | How part 2's versions draw: Alt art re-seeds the card's painter in the normal frame, keeping its subject; the Sketch draws the normal picture in graphite on slate paper, its base frame a pencilled outline; Alt full art is Alt art's picture full bleed; Radiant keeps every mark and no sketch colour is gold (issue #2940, part 2; amends R2725004) | [R2940001](rulings/R2940001.md) | §14.2; R277, R503, R1017001, R2170000, R2202000, R2249000, R2511000, R2724000, R2725000, R2725004, R2949000 |
| R2940002 | Animated, a foil, a finish and a border layer on Alt art, the Sketch and Alt full art; Signature on none of them, by R2949000's rule, refused by the picker and the server; no other pairing is ruled out (issue #2940, part 2; amends R2725003) | [R2940002](rulings/R2940002.md) | §14.2; R1295000, R2715001, R2720000, R2725003, R2949000 |
| R2942000 | Part 3 appends three Epic versions, each offered on a seeded share of cards of its own: Extended art on Spells and Legendary and Mythic cards, Pixel on Rare and Epic Units and Traps and Legendary and Mythic cards, Comic on Rare and Epic Units, Spells and Traps and Legendary and Mythic cards; granted to no account, and the scale stays Common to Mythic (issue #2942, part 3; amends R2725000, R2725001) | [R2942000](rulings/R2942000.md) | §14.2, §14.12; R2725000, R2725001, R2725002, R2940000, R2942001, R2949000, R2949001 |
| R2942001 | How part 3's versions draw: Extended art runs the card's own picture past its window behind a translucent frame and rules box; Pixel samples the normal picture into 8-bit cells in a retro frame, its name in a pixel face; Comic flattens it to pop art with halftone dots and ink outlines, its name in a burst; Radiant keeps every mark and no new colour is gold (issue #2942, part 3; amends R2725004) | [R2942001](rulings/R2942001.md) | §14.2; R277, R503, R1017001, R2170000, R2202000, R2249000, R2718000, R2720000, R2723000, R2724000, R2725000, R2725004, R2940001 |
| R2942002 | Animated, a foil, a finish and a border layer on Extended art, Pixel and Comic; Signature on none of them, by R2949000's rule, refused by the picker and the server; no other pairing is ruled out (issue #2942, part 3; amends R2725003) | [R2942002](rulings/R2942002.md) | §14.2; R1295000, R2715001, R2720000, R2725003, R2940002, R2949000 |
| R2943000 | Part 4 appends two Legendary versions, each a chase offered on a seeded share of cards of its own: Set showcase on Legendary and Mythic cards, Out of frame on Rare to Mythic Units but the Legacy cards and the Starship; granted to no account, and the scale stays Common to Mythic (issue #2943, part 4; amends R2725000, R2725001) | [R2943000](rulings/R2943000.md) | §14.2, §14.12; R2249000, R2724000, R2725000, R2725001, R2725002, R2940000, R2942000, R2943001, R2949000, R2949001 |
| R2943001 | How part 4's versions draw: Set showcase draws the card in its set's own look, its picture in its set's treatment and on a base face its set's edge, bezel and name banner, Classic+ and Meditative+ in their parents'; Out of frame cuts the picture's subject out of a smaller window over the frame, inside the card's rim and under its gem, crest, name, stats and rules box; Radiant keeps every mark and no new colour is gold (issue #2943, part 4; amends R2725004) | [R2943001](rulings/R2943001.md) | §14.2; R277, R503, R2170000, R2202000, R2249000, R2715001, R2715002, R2718000, R2723000, R2724000, R2725000, R2725004, R2940001, R2942001 |
| R2943002 | Animated, a foil, a finish and a border layer on Set showcase and Out of frame; Signature on neither, by R2949000's rule, refused by the picker and the server; no other pairing is ruled out (issue #2943, part 4; amends R2725003) | [R2943002](rulings/R2943002.md) | §14.2; R1295000, R1953001, R2715001, R2725000, R2725003, R2940002, R2942002, R2949000 |
| R2944000 | Part 5 appends two Mythic versions, the top chases, each offered on a seeded share of the marquee cards of its own: Immersive on Legendary and Mythic cards but the Starship, Prismatic on every Mythic card and a share of the Legendary ones; granted to no account, no token buys one, and the scale stays Common to Mythic (issue #2944, part 5; amends R2725000, R2725001) | [R2944000](rulings/R2944000.md) | §14.2, §14.12; R1301002, R2249000, R2725000, R2725001, R2725002, R2728000, R2940000, R2942000, R2943000, R2944001, R2949000, R2949001 |
| R2944001 | How part 5's versions draw: Immersive lays the card's subject on a near layer over its backdrop hazed as far off, inside its window; Prismatic draws its picture in mirror chrome under an iridescent sheen and on a base face a rainbow edge, a chrome bezel and a chrome banner, chrome and never gold; Radiant keeps every mark and no new colour is gold (issue #2944, part 5; amends R2725004) | [R2944001](rulings/R2944001.md) | §14.2; R277, R1295000, R2249000, R2715001, R2723000, R2724000, R2725000, R2725004, R2940001, R2942001, R2943001 |
| R2944002 | Animated, a foil, a finish and a border layer on Immersive and Prismatic; Signature on neither, by R2949000's rule, refused by the picker and the server; Immersive's near layer follows the pointer and the tilt and breathes in a motion slot, the one Animated shares on its face, and holds still under reduced motion, with Animated foil off and on a lazy face; no other pairing is ruled out (issue #2944, part 5; amends R2725003) | [R2944002](rulings/R2944002.md) | §14.2; R1295000, R1953001, R2715001, R2725000, R2725003, R2943002, R2949000 |

| R3013000 | A card's proficiency counts the seat's own dealt plays from hand in ladder, unranked and Conquest games, in the result's transaction; practice, co-op, bots, Created cards and tokens count nothing, nothing is backfilled, and the five levels' curve is 3, 15, 40, 100 and 200 plays, never falling (issue #3013) | [R3013000](rulings/R3013000.md) | §14.8, §14.11, §14.14; R1454, R1502000, R2089000, issue #3013 |

| R3015000 | A card's look is a variant, one picture, and each variant a ladder of levels, each the one below it plus one thing: its art levels first (its normal frame, then its full art where it has one), then animated, then signed above a full art alone; every level a `version-…` entry offered where its variant's first level is, the Default variant's first level the normal face; the seven one-art versions get no full-art level, a Legacy card's ladders stop before animated, Alt art's offer takes Alt full art's cards too, and the Animated and Signature augments retire (issue #3015; amends R2725000, R2725001, R2725003, R2940000, R2940002, R2942000, R2942002, R2943000, R2943002, R2944000, R2944002, R2949001) | [R3015000](rulings/R3015000.md) | §14.2, §14.12; R1017001, R1295000, R1333000, R2511000, R2715001, R2715002, R2724000, R2725000, R2725001, R2725002, R2725003, R2940000, R2940002, R2942000, R2942002, R2943000, R2943002, R2944000, R2944002, R2949000, R2949001, R3015001, R3015002, R3015003, R3015005, R3015006 |
| R3015001 | A level's rarity: a variant's first level, and every level that was a version before, keeps its rarity; each new level is a tier rarer than the level below it, up to Mythic, and a signed level is always Mythic; so the Default's Animated is Epic, as the augment was, and the Sketch's full art Epic; no token buys a Mythic level (issue #3015; amends R2725000, R2942000, R2943000, R2944000) | [R3015001](rulings/R3015001.md) | §14.2, §14.5, §14.6; R1305003, R1333000, R1339000, R2725000, R2728000, R2940000, R2942000, R2943000, R2944000, R3015000, R3015004 |
| R3015002 | Every level of a variant draws its first level's picture: a level adds only the full-art frame, its picture's own motion or a signature, so a signed level is its variant's full art, moving, signed; R2949000's Signature look, a picture and a motion of its own, is gone and nothing is redrawn; the Sketch's full art draws the sketch across the face; foils, finishes and borders layer on every level, and no level wears Radiant's gold (issue #3015; amends R2725003, R2725004, R2940001, R2940002, R2942001, R2942002, R2943001, R2943002, R2944001, R2944002, R2949000) | [R3015002](rulings/R3015002.md) | §14.2; R277, R1017001, R1295000, R2715001, R2715002, R2720000, R2723000, R2724000, R2725003, R2725004, R2940001, R2940002, R2942001, R2942002, R2943001, R2943002, R2944001, R2944002, R2949000, R3015000, R3015005 |
| R3015003 | A pull grants a variant's next level, every level below it owned; where a level past the next is open at the rolled tier, one draw below TIER_WEIGHT_TOTAL comes first, and below LEVEL_SKIP_WEIGHT (40 in 1000) the pull grants such a level with every level below it it lacks; a seeded simulation holds out-of-order pulls to LEVEL_SKIP_MAX_PERCENT_OF_IN_ORDER (10%) of in-order ones, and both odds list the chance (issue #3015; amends R1301002, R1306000) | [R3015003](rulings/R3015003.md) | §14.3, §14.4, §14.6, §14.11; R1301002, R1306000, R1306001, R1339000, R1339002, R1661001, R1665000, R2725001, R2725002, R3015000, R3015006 |
| R3015004 | A cosmetic token or the Chase Shard bar buys only a variant's next level: aimed at a level some level below which the account does not own on that card, it is a 400, "that level needs every level below it owned first", writing and spending nothing; a retired full art or augment is a 400, "that cosmetic is retired" (issue #3015; amends R1305003, R1306002) | [R3015004](rulings/R3015004.md) | §14.5, §14.6, §14.11; R1305003, R1306002, R1953001, R2725000, R2725001, R2728000, R3015000, R3015003, R3015006, R3015007 |
| R3015005 | A card's pick names one level in `fullArt`: `false` the normal face, `true` Full art's first level on a full-art card, or a level's id, offered on the card and owned there; a retired full art's id or a non-empty `augments` is a 400; every older stored pick maps to the level it drew, R2949000's Signature look to Full art's signed level and Alt art or the Sketch with Animated to its first level, and a match freezes one level by its id and no augments (issue #3015; amends R2655000, R2725000, R2949000) | [R3015005](rulings/R3015005.md) | §14.2, §14.12; R1108000, R1310004, R1834000, R1834001, R1834002, R1834003, R1834004, R2655000, R2715000, R2725000, R2725001, R2725002, R2949000, R3015000, R3015002, R3015006 |
| R3015006 | Every retired full art and the Animated augment count as owning the levels they map to on their card, and migration 0084 writes the fifteen new levels and turns every combination an account owns on a card into those levels, each with a ledger row, inserting only; retired rows stay; Signature without both Full art and Animated owns no level, and compensating it is the owner's call (issue #3015; amends R2655000, R2725002) | [R3015006](rulings/R3015006.md) | §14.2, §14.11, §14.12; R1310002, R2655000, R2715000, R2724000, R2725000, R2725002, R2940000, R2949000, R3015000, R3015003, R3015004, R3015005 |
| R3015007 | The Collection shows a card's variants as ladders, one section per variant with its levels lowest first, each owned, next or locked; a locked level past the next names what it needs, only the next is offered a held token, and an owned level is worn by a click; the version and augment strip is gone (issue #3015; amends R2725000, R2949000) | [R3015007](rulings/R3015007.md) | §14.2, §14.5; R1310004, R1953001, R2655000, R2725000, R2725001, R2730000, R2949000, R3015000, R3015002, R3015004, R3015005 |
